HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_04a2c4d9ff93478fbf93","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:7:2\",\"root_000913\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kaybolmanın gizlenme boyutu, sonraki açıklama buyruğuyla birlikte, yol göstermeyi görünmez olanı açığa çıkaran bir hareket olarak da düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B003","root_001583/B001","root_001583/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_05632eecdd5010d4f23a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:7:2\",\"root_000913\",[\"B003\"]],[\"93:7:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Buradaki kaybolmuşluk, öncelikle ahlaki bir damga değil, yerini ve istikametini bulamayan düzeltilebilir bir durum; yol gösterme de bu yönelimin yeniden kurulmasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_230339379fa0a5e5d61b","text":"2. **Hiddenness made visible.** Evidence: ض ل ل B002 (hidden/disappearing/burial), ض ح و B002/B005 in 93:1 (visibility; clear brightness), س م و B002 in 93:0 (raised visible outline), ح د ث B006 in 93:11 (disclosure). Structural cue: the first ayah exposes to light; this ayah names a hidden/lost condition; the final ayah commands disclosure. Mechanism: the packet supplies hiddenness and visibility/disclosure; I supply the revealing arrow. Abductive move: guidance brings the hidden person or meaning into the open. Reading change: \"guided\" becomes also \"made visible,\" not merely instructed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B001","root_001626/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4671041730a2bb6a72f3","text":"1. **Lostness as redirected course.** Evidence: و ج د B001 (finding), ض ل ل B001/B003 (astray from aim; failing to locate), ه د ي B001/B002 (gentle guidance; course/direction). Structural cue: the ayah repeats the find → deficient state → remedy pattern of 93:6. Mechanism: the packet supplies loss and guidance branches; I supply the correctional arrow from mis-aim to course. Abductive move: guidance is not just information but realignment of trajectory. Reading change: \"astray\" reads as having no stable direction, and \"guided\" as being set on a course.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4d2a70027a2dfc2d593c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:7:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yol göstermenin yakın birine gönderilen hediye boyutu, yönü uzaktan verilen kuru bir bilgi değil, ilişki içinde sunulan lütuf haline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001028/B002","root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_725628dc69e03dee94ab","text":"3. **Guidance is itself a gracious gift.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ه د ي:B004` is a gracious gift sent to someone close; 93:5 supplies giving through `ع ط و:B002`, and 93:6 supplies compassionate turning through `ء و ي:B002`. **Structural cue:** promise, shelter, and guidance occur in one care sequence and share the same second-person recipient. **Assigned role:** the gift branch makes direction a relational bestowal rather than bare information. **Abductive move:** I infer common recipient plus ordered care acts → guidance as one mode of giving. **Reading change:** `فَهَدَىٰ` reads as an intimate provision that establishes direction, not merely an instruction delivered from a distance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_79f9c3b8cf1b49a8693b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:7:2\",\"root_000913\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ardışık iki ayetteki başıboş hayvan çağrışımı, kaybolmuşluğu hem yönsüzlük hem de korunmaya muhtaçlık taşıyan somut bir savrulma imgesine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B008","root_001583/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8ba201334e24e5c2255e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:7:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B008\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Destekli yürüyüş ve sakin duruş çağrışımları, yol göstermeyi dengesiz hareketten toparlanmış bir yürüyüşe ve iç sükunete geçiş olarak bedenleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ad7767c98c287bae5621","text":"1. **The local mechanism favors recovery of orientation over moral labeling.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ض ل ل:B003` is losing or failing to locate something, fixed `ه د ي:B001` is gentle guidance toward a way, and fixed `ه د ي:B002` is course and intended direction. **Structural cue:** the ayah stands between the formally parallel orphan/shelter pair (93:6) and need/sufficiency pair (93:8), all introduced by finding and resolved by `فَ`. **Assigned role:** loss of location supplies the deficit; guidance supplies both direction and a course of conduct. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies parallel grammar and practical remedies; I infer that the middle descriptor likewise names a remediable condition rather than serving only as condemnation. **Reading change:** `ضَآلًّا` can foreground being unlocated or without a settled course, with `فَهَدَىٰ` as recovery and orientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B005","root_001068/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae7968353562499bcfdf","text":"2. **Adjacent roots activate a shared stray-dependent image.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ض ل ل:B005` is a stray animal in a place of loss; 93:8 activates `ع ي ل:B008`, a stray animal unsure which direction to seek. **Structural cue:** the two descriptors occur in consecutive `وَوَجَدَكَ ... فَ...` clauses, and each is answered by an enabling verb. **Assigned role:** the stray image makes vulnerability simultaneously directional and dependent. **Abductive move:** I infer that adjacency plus identical syntax licenses the material analogy; the packet does not assert that either surface noun literally denotes an animal here. **Reading change:** the ayah gains an image of a vulnerable wanderer recovered into a viable course, while preserving other readings.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b014d6b91a833d0e962b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:7:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Aynı kökün kutsal yere gönderilen sunu boyutu, yol göstermeye adanmış bir armağan niteliği yüklemeyi önerir; bu törensel çağrışım bağlamda zayıftır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b6702a490b6dec4c5173","text":"3. **Stray animal under a Lord-owner.** Evidence: ض ل ل B005 (stray domestic animal with no known owner), ر ب ب B001 in 93:3/5/11 (owner/master), ء و ي B001 in 93:6 (calling horses back to voice), ع ي ل B008 in 93:8 (stray animal losing direction). Structural cue: adjacent ayat use finding and sheltering; later \"Lord\" recurs. Mechanism: the packet supplies stray-animal branches and Lord-as-owner; I supply the pastoral relation that guidance is the owner's call returning the stray. Abductive move: the absence of known ownership is negated by ربّ. Reading change: 93:7 can be heard as return from ownerless wandering to recognized belonging.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c013cd0432b18d47afb7","text":"93:8 confirmed the stray-animal reading more than expected: ع ي ل B008 repeats the image of a directionless stray immediately after ض ل ل B005. 93:10 and 93:11 then shifted the visibility reading: guidance brings the hidden into the open, but the later commands require that openness become non-rebuking reception and grateful disclosure, not exposure for shame.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c89419b3dd960d6796a2","text":"93:8 created the sharpest surprise by activating fixed `ض ل ل:B005` through its adjacent `ع ي ل:B008`; the shared stray image corrected an exclusively moral reading toward recoverable disorientation and dependence. At 93:11, `ح د ث:B006` added a second retrospective relation: what had been hidden or unlocated can, after guidance, be brought out into report.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dcc4867ee3f0eb978d99","text":"4. **Guidance as gracious gift/offering.** Evidence: ه د ي B004 (gift sent to someone close), ه د ي B005 (offering sent to sanctuary), ع ط و B002 in 93:5 (giving), ن ع م B001 in 93:11 (favor). Structural cue: the future gift in 93:5 and final favor in 93:11 surround the guidance recollection. Mechanism: the packet supplies gift/offering branches in ه د ي and giving/favor branches elsewhere; I supply the classification of guidance as a bestowed gift. Abductive move: a path can be given like a gift, not merely pointed out. Reading change: \"He guided\" reads as a gracious bestowal and consecrated sending, preserving a devotional/ritual overtone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B003","root_001583/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fab566dcb582d40df9a3","text":"5. **Composed walking after weakness.** Evidence: ه د ي B008/B010 (supported walking; calm composed bearing), ع ي ل B003 in 93:8 (swaying gait), س ج و B001/B003 in 93:2 (still settling; disposition). Structural cue: the motion/direction verse sits between shelter and enrichment. Mechanism: the packet supplies gait and stillness branches; I supply the embodiment that guidance stabilizes an unsteady walker. Abductive move: losing direction is also bodily instability, and guidance gives bearing. Reading change: \"هدى\" is not only cognitive; it is a restored composure of movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
